TITLE: API Examples for Pagination
DESCRIPTION: Examples demonstrating how to apply offset-based and page-based pagination using GET requests with specific query parameters for the workflow tasks endpoint.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/get-tasks

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
GET workflow/v1/tasks?start=0&limit=20
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
GET workflow/v1/tasks?page=0&size=20
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: API Offset-Based Pagination Example
DESCRIPTION: Demonstrates how to implement offset-based pagination in API requests. This method uses 'start' to define the initial record index and 'limit' to specify the maximum number of records to retrieve per request.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/get-subtask-templates

LANGUAGE: Shell
CODE:
```
GET /workflow/v1/templates/task/items/{taskTemplateId}/subtasks?start=0&limit=20
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Shell Command Example: Get eFolder Document
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates the basic Shell command structure to invoke the 'Get a Document' API endpoint. This command directly uses the 'get' method with the full API path, requiring replacement of placeholder IDs.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/get-document

LANGUAGE: Shell
CODE:
```
get https://api.elliemae.com/encompass/v1/loans/{loanId}/documents/{documentId}
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: V3 Get External Organization API: Fee Setup Filters
DESCRIPTION: The V3 Get External Organization API has been enhanced to return Fee Setup information, including TPO Fees collection and Late Fees settings. New 'entities' filters are added to specify the type of fee setup information to retrieve.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/release-notes

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API: V3 Get External Organization
  Description: Enhanced to return Fee Setup information.
  New 'entities' filters for Fee Setup:
    - TpoFees: Retrieve TPO Fees collection.
    - LateFees: Retrieve Late Fees settings.
    - FeeSetup: Retrieve both TPO Fees and Late Fees.
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Make a V3 Get Loan API Call using cURL
DESCRIPTION: This example illustrates how to make a service call to the Encompass V3 Get Loan API. It uses the previously obtained access token for authorization and includes the 'loanId' as a path parameter to retrieve details of a specific loan record. Query parameters can also be used to limit the API response.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/sdk-to-api-migration-getting-started-guide

LANGUAGE: bash
CODE:
```
curl --location 'https://api.elliemae.com/encompass/v3/loans/{{loanId}}' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer {{access_tToken}}'
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: API Enhancement: V3 Get External Organization with Fee Setup Filters
DESCRIPTION: The V3 Get External Organization API has been enhanced to return Fee Setup information, including TPO Fees collection and Late Fees settings. New filters 'TpoFees', 'LateFees', and 'FeeSetup' (which includes both) have been added to retrieve this data.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/changelog

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
V3 Get External Organization API Enhancements:
  New 'entities' filters for Fee Setup:
    - TpoFees: Returns TPO Fees collection.
    - LateFees: Returns Late Fees settings.
    - FeeSetup: Returns both TpoFees and LateFees.
```

TITLE: JavaScript Loan Object Initialization and Methods
DESCRIPTION: Demonstrates how to initialize the loan object and provides JavaScript examples for interacting with it. Covers retrieving the entire loan, getting and setting specific fields, checking read-only status, triggering calculations, and merging workspace changes.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/ellie-mae-scripting-objects-reference

LANGUAGE: JavaScript
CODE:
```
const loanObject = await elli.script.getObject('loan');
// get entire loan object
const responseFromHostWithLoan = await loanObject.all();
console.log('Here is your loan response from host: ', responseFromHostWithLoan);

// To get value of field with fieldId 4002, you would do:
const fieldValue = await loanObject.getField(4002);
console.log('FieldId 4002 has value: ', fieldValue);
await loanObject.setFields(fieldIdMap);

// To set fieldId 4002 to be "Justin", you would do :
const fieldMap = { '4002': 'Justin' };
await loanObject.setFields(fieldMap);

// Check the loan is read only
const isReadOnly = await loanObject.isReadOnly();
console.log('Loan is read only: ', resp);
// trigger calculations
await loanObject.calculate();
// sync the loan workspace with any changes made by other users
await loanObject.merge();
```

TITLE: Get Current Borrower Pair and Subscribe to Changes in SSF
DESCRIPTION: This snippet shows how to get the current borrower pair using `getCurrentApplication()` and how to subscribe to the 'applicationselected' event on the loan object to be alerted to changes in the borrower pair. It also provides an example of the returned object structure.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/the-ellie-mae-scripting-framework

LANGUAGE: javascript
CODE:
```
loan.getCurrentApplication()

// This returns the following object as return value:

{
  "index": 0,
  "id": "17cfe1b6-e440-4806-a948-8dd960e112b0",
  "legacyId": "_borrower1"
}
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Retrieve Loan Data using JavaScript
DESCRIPTION: Illustrates how to make a GET request to the Developer Connect loans API to retrieve specific loan data for a given loan GUID. Requires an access token passed as a Bearer token in the request header.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/how-to-get-loan-data-by-making-calls-to-developer-connect-apis

LANGUAGE: JavaScript
CODE:
```
var loanGuid = '61d0c054-4528-4d6e-a20c-048b82cd10c5';
var url = 'https://api.elliemae.com/encompass/v3/loans/<loanguid>';
var loanUrl = url.replace('<loanguid>', loanGuid);

$.ajax({
url: loanUrl,
success: function(response) {
console.log("Success, Loan Data ===", response.GetLoanResponse.LoanData);
},

beforeSend: function (xhr) {
xhr.setRequestHeader ("Authorization", "Bearer 7JkdjsLowrjfjDorirMcvnXz"));
},
type: 'GET',
contentType: 'json',
});
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: API Enhancements: Pagination and View Options for eFolder Documents
DESCRIPTION: The V3 Get List of Documents and V3 Get a Document APIs now support pagination via 'start' and 'limit' query parameters, and a 'view' parameter with 'Summary', 'Detail', and 'Full' values. The 'Full' view allows retrieval of comments. Additionally, 'lastAttachmentDate' and 'updatedDate' read-only attributes are now supported.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/changelog

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API: V3 Get List of Documents, V3 Get a Document\nQuery Parameters:\n  start: Pagination start index (V3 Get List of Documents only)\n  limit: Pagination limit (V3 Get List of Documents only), default returns all records\n  view: Document detail level\n    Values: Summary, Detail (default), Full (includes comments)\nNew Read-Only Attributes:\n  lastAttachmentDate\n  updatedDate
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: V3 External Organizations API Updates
DESCRIPTION: Several V3 External Organizations APIs have received updates. The Get External Organization API now includes a new query parameter for specifying entities, and new contract attributes have been added to support TPOC Setup, Commitments, and Loan Criteria. Additionally, Get External Organizations and Get an External Organization APIs now return summary information instead of errors when users lack proper access rights.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/changelog

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:

TITLE: eFolder Documents APIs: Pagination, View Options, and New Attributes
DESCRIPTION: The V3 Get List of Documents and V3 Get a Document APIs have been enhanced. Pagination is introduced with 'start' and 'limit' parameters. A new 'view' parameter allows retrieving Summary, Detail (default), or Full (including comments) document views. Additionally, 'lastAttachmentDate' and 'updatedDate' are now supported as read-only attributes.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/release-notes

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API: V3 Get List of Documents, V3 Get a Document

Enhancements:
- Pagination:
  Query Parameters: 'start', 'limit'
  Default 'limit': All records
- View Parameter:
  Query Parameter: 'view'
  Values: Summary, Detail (default), Full (includes comments)
- New Read-Only Attributes:
  'lastAttachmentDate'
  'updatedDate'
```

TITLE: Enhance eFolder Documents APIs with Pagination and View Options
DESCRIPTION: The V3 Get List of Documents and V3 Get a Document APIs have been enhanced to improve performance and data retrieval. Pagination parameters 'start' and 'limit' are introduced for listing documents, while a 'view' parameter allows specifying the level of detail (Summary, Detail, Full) for both document retrieval APIs. Additionally, 'lastAttachmentDate' and 'updatedDate' read-only attributes are now supported.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/changelog/252-major-release

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API: V3 Get List of Documents
  Query Parameters:
    - start: For pagination
    - limit: For pagination (default: all records)
    - view: (Summary, Detail, Full) - default Detail
  Supported Read-Only Attributes: lastAttachmentDate, updatedDate

API: V3 Get a Document
  Query Parameters:
    - view: (Summary, Detail, Full) - default Detail
  Supported Read-Only Attributes: lastAttachmentDate, updatedDate

Note: 'view' equal to Full now retrieves list of comments.
```

TITLE: API Documentation for User Creation
DESCRIPTION: This section provides comprehensive API documentation for the user creation endpoint, covering the necessary query parameters, the expected JSON structure for the request body, and detailed descriptions for each field within the user object, including data types, constraints, and examples.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/sdk-migration-encompass-sdk-to-rest-apis

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
PARAMETER | DESCRIPTION
orgid string | Required. Unique identifier of the organization to create the user(s) under.
action string | Required. To create users, the action is "add".
view string | Optional. Possible value is "entity".
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
[
  {
    "useCompanyAddress": true,
    "firstName": "string",
    "middleName": "string",
    "lastName": "string",
    "suffix": "string",
    "title": "string",
    "address": "string",
    "city": "string",
    "state": "AL",
    "zipCode": "string",
    "phone": "string",
    "cellPhone": "string",
    "fax": "string",
    "email": "string",
    "nmlsId": "string",
    "nmlsCurrent": true,
    "ssn": "string",
    "useParentInfoForRateLock": true,
    "emailForRateSheet": "string",
    "faxForRateSheet": "string",
    "emailForLockInfo": "string",
    "faxForLockInfo": "string",
    "emailForLogin": "string",
    "siteUrls": [
      "string"
    ],
    "salesRep": {
      "entityId": "string",
      "entityName": "string",
      "entityUri": "string",
      "entityType": "Default"
    },
    "personas": [
      {
        "entityId": "string",
        "entityName": "string",
        "entityUri": "string",
        "entityType": "Default"
      }
    ],
    "peerLoanAccess": "Disabled",
    "accessMode": "ReadWrite",
    "licenses": [
      {
        "selected": true,
        "stateAbbrevation": "AL",
        "licenseNumber": "string",
        "issueDate": "2021-12-14T22:58:29.628Z",
        "startDate": "2021-12-14T22:58:29.628Z",
        "endDate": "2021-12-14T22:58:29.628Z",
        "licenseStatusType": "TransitionRequested",
        "statusDate": "2021-12-14T22:58:29.628Z",
        "lastChecked": "2021-12-14T22:58:29.628Z"
      }
    ],
    "notes": "string"
  }
]
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
ATTRIBUTE | DESCRIPTION | LENGTH/FORMAT
useCompanyAddress boolean | Required. Indicates if the address of the user will be inherited from the parent company. | true/false
firstName string | Required. External user’s first name. | 64
middleName string | External user’s middle name. | 64
lastName string | Required. External user’s lastname. | 64
suffix string | Suffix for the external user. | 64
title string | External user’s professional title. | 64
address string | Address of the external user. Should not be provided in the payload if useCompanyAddress is set as true. | 255
city string | City of the external user. Should not be provided in the payload if useCompanyAddress is set as true. | 50
state string | State of the external user. Should not be provided in the payload if useCompanyAddress is set as true. | State permitted abbrev enum   AL, AK, AZ, AR, CA, CO, CT, DC, DE, FL,GA, HI, ID, IL, IN, IA, KS, KY, LA, ME,MD, MA, MI, MN, MS, MO, MT, NE, NV, NH,NJ, NM, NY, NC, ND, OH, OK, OR, PA, RI,SC, SD, TN, TX, UT, VT, VA, WA, WV, WI,WY, VI, GU, PR
zipCode string | Zip code of the external user. Should not be provided in the payload if useCompanyAddress is set as true. | 5 digits US Zip Code with optional 4 digits extension in the format #####-####.
phone string | External user’s phone number. | 10 digits Phone Number with an optional 1-4 digits extension in the format ###-###-#### ####."
cellPhone string | External user’s cell phone number. | 10 digits Phone Number with an optional 1-4 digits extension in the format ###-###-#### ####."
fax string | Fax number for the external user. | 10 digits Phone Number with an optional 1-4 digits extension in the format ###-###-#### ####."
email string | Required. Email of the external user. The value must be in the required format (@, .com or .org, etc...) | 64 in email format
nmlsId string | License number of Nationwide Multistate Licensing System. | 12
nmlsCurrent boolean | Indicates whether the external user’s NMLS is current. | true/false
ssn string | Social Security Number of the external user. | 9 digits Tax Id in the format ##-####### or Social Security Number in the format ###-##-####.
useParentInfoForRateLock boolean | Required. Indicates if the details of Rate sheet and Lock info will be inherited from parent Org. | true/false
emailForRateSheet string | Email address to which the system will send the Rate Sheet. | 64 in email format
faxForRateSheet string | Fax number to which the system will send the Rate Sheet. | 10 digits Phone Number with an optional 1-4 digits extension in the format ###-###-#### ####."
emailForLockInfo string | Email address to which the system will send the Lock information. | 64 in email format
faxForLockInfo string | Fax number to which the system will send the Lock information. | 10 digits Phone Number with an optional 1-4 digits extension in the format ###-###-#### ####."
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: API Behavior Change: Restricted Editing of Read-Only Fields (V1/V3 Loan APIs)
DESCRIPTION: Starting with the 24.1 release, V1 and V3 Loan APIs will restrict editing of fields where `allowEdit=0` (or `readOnly: true` as returned by the V3 Get Field Schema API). This means fields identified as read-only via the Get Field Schema API can no longer be modified through these loan APIs. This change was deferred to a future release.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/changelog/233-major-release

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API Change: V1/V3 All Loan APIs (EBSP-40379)
  Description: Restrict editing of fields with allowEdit=0.
  Impact: Fields with "readOnly: true" from V3 Get Field Schema API can no longer be edited.
  Status: Deferred to a future release.
```

TITLE: Fetch Metadata for New Standard Fields using V3 Get Field Schema API
DESCRIPTION: This snippet demonstrates how to retrieve metadata, including the JSON path, for a newly added standard field in the Encompass Loan Schema using the V3 Get Field Schema API. The example uses a cURL command with a specific field ID and shows the expected JSON response payload.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/update-data-dictionary-with-changes-to-the-encompass-loan-schema

LANGUAGE: cURL
CODE:
```
curl --location 'https://{{API_SERVER}}/encompass/v3/schemas/loan/standardFields?
start=0&limit=10000&ids=4000' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer {{ACCESS_TOKEN}}'
```

LANGUAGE: JSON
CODE:
```
[
    {
      "id": "4000",
      "description": "Borrower First Name",
      "format": "STRING",
      "readOnly": false,
      "nullable": true,
      "category": "Borrower",
      "dataType": "String",
      "maxLength": 0,
      "multiInstance": false,
      "contractPath": "loan.currentApplication.borrower.firstName",
      "jsonPath": "$.applications[0].borrower.firstName",
      "entitiesFilterKey": "borrower"
    }
]
```

TITLE: Example JSON Payload for Rate Lock Adjustments Update
DESCRIPTION: This JSON payload demonstrates how to update various rate lock adjustments within a `lockRequest` object. It includes examples for `lockNumberOfDays`, `lockExpirationDate`, `lockDate`, and an array of `adjustments` with different types and values. The example also shows `totalRateAdjustments` and `netRate` fields.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/product-and-pricing-setting-the-rate

LANGUAGE: JSON
CODE:
```
{
  "lockRequest": {
    "lockNumberOfDays": 365,
    "lockExpirationDate": "2024-01-20",
    "lockDate": "2023-01-20",
    "adjustments": [
      {
        "adjustmentType": "Adjustment",
        "description": "15 Day Lock Period",
        "priceAdjustmentType": "BaseRate",
        "adjustment": 1
      },
      {
        "adjustmentType": "Adjustment",
        "description": "45 Day Lock Period",
        "priceAdjustmentType": "BaseRate",
        "adjustment": 2
      },
      {
        "adjustmentType": "Adjustment",
        "description": "60 Day Lock Period",
        "priceAdjustmentType": "BaseRate",
        "adjustment": 3
      },
      {
        "adjustmentType": "Adjustment",
        "description": "Fixed: LTV <= 70.00%",
        "priceAdjustmentType": "BaseRate",
        "adjustment": 4
      },
      {
        "adjustmentType": "Adjustment",
        "description": "ARM: LTV 80.01 - 85.00%",
        "priceAdjustmentType": "BaseRate",
        "adjustment": 5
      },
      {
        "adjustmentType": "Adjustment",
        "description": "ARM: LTV 85.01 - 90.00%",
        "priceAdjustmentType": "BaseRate",
        "adjustment": 6
      }
    ],
    "totalRateAdjustments": 21,
    "netRate": 21
  }
}
```

TITLE: Include Script Tag for Scripting Framework Guest Library
DESCRIPTION: To enable a guest application to interact with the host, it must load and initialize the Scripting Framework Guest Library. This JavaScript library provides functions for retrieving scripting objects. The recommended approach is to include a script tag referencing the library from ICE Mortgage Technology's web servers, preferably in the <head> section of the page. Two versions are available: 'elli.ssf.guest-with-polyfill.js' for modern JavaScript features and 'elli.ssf.guest.js' if a polyfill library is already included.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/custom-tools

LANGUAGE: JavaScript
CODE:
```
<script src="https://cdn.elliemae.io/elliemae/core/ssf/1.0/elli.ssf.gue st-with-polyfill.js"></script>
```

TITLE: Assign Loan Associate using PATCH API with cURL
DESCRIPTION: This cURL command demonstrates how to update a loan milestone to assign a loan associate. It uses a PATCH request to the Encompass V3 API, specifying the start date and associate details such as type, user ID, and contact information.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/sdk-to-api-migration-getting-started-guide

LANGUAGE: bash
CODE:
```
curl --location --request PATCH 'https://qa.api.ellielabs.com/encompass/v3/loans/1a88e27a-7d12-44b9-8eb4-5258c05c1d8a/milestones/4f049bab-dc59-40b1-a8f9-199babe21022' \
--header 'Accept: application/json' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer 0004lwov92ap4LvlSg7HAFtqvXDz' \
--data '{
    "startDate": "2024-03-28T10:51:00Z",
    "loanAssociate": {
        "loanAssociateType": "User",
        "user": {
            "entityId": "underwriter",
            "entityType": "User"
        },
        "cellPhone": "124-567-1258",
        "email": {{EMAIL_ADDR}},
        "fax": "123-567-1234",
        "phone": "123-567-1234"
    }
}'
```

TITLE: JavaScript: Initialize and Use Auth Object
DESCRIPTION: This snippet demonstrates how to retrieve the 'auth' object from the 'elli.script' context and then use its methods to get user data and an access token. It shows how to log the retrieved information to the console.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/ellie-mae-scripting-objects-reference

LANGUAGE: JavaScript
CODE:
```
const authObject = await elli.script.getObject('auth');
const userData = await authObject.getUser();
console.log('Here is your user data from host: ', userData);
const { access_token } = await authObject.getAccessToken();
console.log('Accesstoken from Host:', access_token);
```

TITLE: API Pagination Query Parameters
DESCRIPTION: Demonstrates how to implement offset-based and page-based pagination using specific query parameters in API requests. Offset-based pagination uses 'start' and 'limit', while page-based pagination uses 'page' and 'size'.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/get-all-templates

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
GET workflow/v1/tasks/items?start=0&limit=20
```

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
GET workflow/v1/tasks/items?page=0&size=20
```

TITLE: Example Freddie Mac Affordable Check API Request Body
DESCRIPTION: This JSON snippet provides a sample request body for the Freddie Mac Affordable Check API. It demonstrates the structure and required fields for submitting a new order, including product details, entity references, credentials, and options.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/freddie-mac-affordable-check

LANGUAGE: JSON
CODE:
```
{
	"product": {
		"entityRef": {
			"entityId": "{loan_uid}",
			"entityType": "urn:elli:encompass:loan"
		},
		"name": "AUS-LPAffordableCheck",
		"credentials": {
			"userName": "{username}",
			"password": "{password}",
			"tpoAccountIdentifier": "{TPO ID if applicable}",
			"sellerServicerNumber": "{seller #}"
		},
		"options": {
			"requestType": "NewOrder",
			"requestId": "{GUID generated by caller}",
			"partyRoleType": "{Broker | Seller | Servicer | Correspondent | Lender }"
		},
		"clientEntity": {
			"configName": "{set 'UAT' point to Freddie Mac lower environment - leave blank for Prod}"
		}
	}
}
```

TITLE: Overview of Available API Services and Endpoints
DESCRIPTION: This documentation snippet provides a structured overview of the API services offered, detailing the main service categories and their respective endpoints. It serves as an index to the full API documentation for each service, outlining the available operations like getting reports, managing user mappings, and retrieving product and pricing information.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/get-canonical-names

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Encompass Compliance Service:
  - Contracts
  - Get a Compliance Report (GET)
  - Get Compliance Reports (GET)
  - Create a Compliance Report (POST)
ICE Product and Pricing Engine (ICE PPE):
  - Contracts
  - Get User Mapping (GET)
  - Map ICE PPE User (PATCH)
  - Get Programs and Rates (POST)
  - Get Eligible Rates (POST)
  - Select Rate (POST)
  - Get Adjustments (POST)
  - Get Guideline Details (GET)
  - Get Agency Approvals (GET)
  - Get Bankruptcy (GET)
  - Get Buydown Type (GET)
  - Get Buydown Contributor Type (GET)
  - Get Citizenship (GET)
  - Get Client Settings (GET)
  - Get Commitment Types (GET)
  - Get Counties (GET)
  - Get Custom Fields (GET)
  - Get Delivery Types (GET)
```

TITLE: Partner Services API Enhancement for Use Cases
DESCRIPTION: The Partner Services API (Credit Partners and Samples) has been enhanced to support a new 'Use Cases' customer setup request option. This option allows Lenders to define what options should be checked/unchecked or locked/unlocked on the Encompass partner integration user interface, replacing dynamic UI configuration. The API remains backward compatible if 'Use Cases' is not utilized.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/release-notes

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
Partner Services API (Credit Partners and Samples):
  Enhancement: Supports new 'Use Cases' customer setup request option.
  Purpose: Allows Lenders to define UI configurations for Encompass partner integration.
  Note: Replaces dynamic user interface configuration. No breaking change if not used.
```

TITLE: eClose Integration APIs Overview
DESCRIPTION: Overview of the key APIs provided by ICE Mortgage Technology Point of Sale Framework for eClose integration, including Create Package Event and Create Session.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/encompass-eclose-integration

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
eClose Integration APIs:
  Create Package Event:
    Description: Creates an eClose package event. A synchronous webhook listener allows the Delivery Partner integration to hook into package creation and observe data, replace email content, and add a link to the portal.
  Create Session:
    Description: Creates the Delivery Room session and UI that point of sale recipients will use to view, provide electronic consent, electronically sign, and complete eClose packages.
```

TITLE: Encompass SDK Common Operations to API Alternatives
DESCRIPTION: This section provides a mapping of common Encompass SDK operations to their equivalent Encompass Developer Connect (EDC) API alternatives, indicating API support and providing references to detailed API documentation.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/sdk-to-api-migration-getting-started-guide

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
SDK Operation & Method: Create, Read, Update, Delete Loan
  EDC API Supported: Yes
  API Alternative: Loan Management APIs

SDK Operation & Method: Assign a loan officer and/or loan processor
  EDC API Supported: Yes. Also can manage loan milestones and milestone free roles.
  API Alternative: Associates & Milestones APIs

SDK Operation & Method: Import data in standard formats
  EDC API Supported: Yes – ILAD, ULAD
  API Alternative: Import from File APIs

SDK Operation & Method: Export data in standard format
  EDC API Supported: Yes – UCD, ULDD
  API Alternative: Export Loan to MISMO 3.4 API

SDK Operation & Method: Read Loan Folders
  EDC API Supported: Yes
  API Alternative: Loan Folder APIs

SDK Operation & Method: Create, Read Update, Delete Borrower Contacts
  EDC API Supported: Yes
  API Alternative: Borrower Contacts APIs

SDK Operation & Method: Create, Read Update, Delete Business Contacts
  EDC API Supported: Yes, except for custom fields (Plan 25.1)
  API Alternative: Business Contacts APIs

SDK Operation & Method: Complex queries against loan pipeline.
  EDC API Supported: Yes
  API Alternative: Loan Pipeline APIs

SDK Operation & Method: Complex queries against contacts.
  EDC API Supported: Yes
  API Alternative: Borrower Contacts APIs, Business Contact APIs, Group Contacts APIs
```

TITLE: Plugins 2.0 Initiative: Encompass Web Plugins Integration with Desktop
DESCRIPTION: This notice introduces the Plugins 2.0 initiative, aimed at modernizing Encompass and assisting with the SDK Transition. It enables clients to integrate Encompass web plugins with the desktop version, supports JavaScript for coding, and provides a new Plugin 2.0 Trace Viewer for troubleshooting.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/changelog

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
// Plugins 2.0 Initiative Key Capabilities:

- Run Encompass web plugins within Encompass desktop.
- Use JavaScript for coding plugins (modern, widely supported scripting language).
- Access new Plugin 2.0 Trace Viewer (in Encompass desktop Help menu) for real-time message/event troubleshooting.
- Augment Encompass SDK Transition work efforts.

// Note: Not all Encompass web interface plugin features are supported in desktop, and vice versa, at this time.
// Contact ICE Mortgage Technology relationship manager for participation.
```

TITLE: Print PDF or JPG File (JavaScript)
DESCRIPTION: Provides a JavaScript example for printing a PDF or JPG file using the `appObj.print` method. It fetches the file as a blob and passes it to the print function, noting that the method returns immediately and does not wait for the print operation to complete.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/ellie-mae-scripting-objects-reference

LANGUAGE: JavaScript
CODE:
```
async function btnPrint_click(ctrl) {
  try {
    const data = await fetch('<URL_TO_PDF_OR_JPG_FILE>');
    const blob = await data.blob();
    const printOptions = { blob };
    const appObj = await elli.script.getObject('application');
    await appObj.print(printOptions);
  } catch (err) {
  	console.log('Error occurred while printing ', err);
  }
}
```

TITLE: Example Request Payload for Loan Action
DESCRIPTION: Provides an example of the JSON payload structure used when invoking a loan action with pending changes. It shows how 'merge' operations and 'performAction' are combined within a single request.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/ellie-mae-scripting-objects-reference

LANGUAGE: JSON
CODE:
```
[
{
"op":"merge",
"value":"35000", "contractPath":"loan.fhaVaLoan.energyEfficientMortgageAmount"
},
{
"op":"performAction","value":"calculateEEMMortgage"
}
]
```

TITLE: Point of Sale Framework API Implementation Errors
DESCRIPTION: This section documents various implementation errors encountered when interacting with the Point of Sale Framework API. It provides details on HTTP status codes, internal error codes (POSF-XXXX), a summary of the issue, and specific example messages to aid in debugging and resolution.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/handling-the-create-package-event-1

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API Errors:
  Bad Request Input:
    Description: Input in the request could not be processed because the format or structure was invalid.
    Status/Code: 400 / POSF-0003
    Summary: Bad format - failed input validation
    Example Details: group.id is required.
  Environment Mismatch:
    Description: The elli-environment header value did not match executing environment.
    Status/Code: 400 / POSF-0004
    Summary: The environment provided in the request must match execution environment.
    Example Details: Request Elli-Environment: {expectedEnvironment} is different from executing environment: {actualEnvironment}.
  Missing Required Authentication Header:
    Description: One or more of the authentication headers was missing. Header names should be treated as case insensitive.
    Status/Code: 401 / POSF-0005
    Summary: Required signing header(s) are missing.
    Example Details: Elli-SigningKeyId
  Subscription Not Found:
    Description: The value provided in the subscription id header is not a recognized Partner Integration id.
    Status/Code: 401 / POSF-0006
    Summary: SubscriptionId provided is not authorized for access.
    Example Details: Elli-SubscriptionId does not exist for Id {subscriptionId}.
  Signing Key Not Found:
    Description: The signing key id passed was not found in the provided integration.
    Status/Code: 401 / POSF-0007
    Summary: SigningKeyId provided does not exist for integration.
    Example Details: Elli-SigningKeyId does not exist for Id {signingKeyIdId}.
  Invalid Authentication:
    Description: The signature provided does not match that calculated by the resolved signing key.
    Status/Code: 401 / POSF-0008
    Summary: Invalid authorization.
    Example Details: Invalid Elli-Signature.
  Invalid Instance:
    Description: The customer instance is not recognized as one supported.
    Status/Code: 403 / POSF-0009
    Summary: Instance not supported by integration.
    Example Details: Instance {instance id} is not supported by subscription {subscription id}.
  Entity Not Found:
    Description: An entity referenced in the payload does not exist as expected. In entities being referenced / validated during processing will ideally be shared with IMT during implementation to be documented for diagnostic purposes.
    Status/Code: 400 / POSF-0010
    Summary: A referenced entity does not exist.
    Example Details: The entity {field value} referenced in {field name} cannot be found as expected.
  Already executed:
    Description: Message was already executed but should not proceed for some reason. One example would be if you know it was already completed.
    Status/Code: 409 / POSF-0011
    Summary: Entity has already been executed and cannot proceed.
    Example Details: The package {id} on {group.namespace} entity with id of {group.id} has already been completed.
  Api Url Not Found:
    Description: Partner api url connects but returns not found. May be partner service issue or misconfiguration on partner setup api root.
    Status/Code: 404
    Summary: N/A
    Example Details: N/A
  Host / Url connection Error:
    Description: The host could not be resolved / reached or the endpoint could not connect. May be partner infrastructure issue or misconfiguration on partner setup api root.
    Status/Code: N/A
    Summary: N/A
```

----------------------------------------

TITLE: External User Creation API Behavior and Constraints
DESCRIPTION: Important notes and behavioral differences for the External User creation API, covering requirements, limitations, and specific parameter handling compared to previous SDK methods. It highlights crucial configuration and data validation aspects.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/sdk-migration-encompass-sdk-to-rest-apis

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
- Unlike the current SDK call, a commit() method is not required to create the External user with the new REST API.
- updatedBy and updatedDate are not allowed to be passed as request parameters for the new REST API.
- Any user with access rights to create External user can create the External user with the new REST API.
- Unlike in SKD method, if user parent info attribute is set as true and the user passes the related attributes in the request payload, the API will fail indicating that the user cannot pass these attributes when the useParentInfo is set as true.
- If the ORG is defined with one site URL and if we create any external user, the site URL will not be inherited unless the parameter is explicitly supplied in the request payload.
- Unlike smart client or SDK call, the API will not create the external user under root org if the ORG ID is missing in the request. If ORG ID is missing in the request, the API will fail.
- It is important to configure Encompass server with Hazelcast Cache. Without which the API may fail due to data mismatch between the cache and the database.
- Unlike SDK method, License status can't be a free text. Hence the API will fail if the License status is not one of the valid value.
- If the supplied URL or Persona or SalesRep user or State license is invalid or any temporary issue with DB connection during the transaction, the external user POST API will fail unlike the Smart client where the error is thrown but the user is created in the system.
- A new attribute 'licenseStatusType' introduces in the API contract which will accept ENUM values for license status.
- API query parameter action will assume default value as update. Hence for Create functionality, the action parameter must be passed with value as add.
```

TITLE: Create Encompass Webhook Subscriptions
DESCRIPTION: Examples of JSON payloads used to create webhook subscriptions with the Encompass Lending Platform. A successful subscription creation returns an HTTP 201 status code, with the subscription ID in the 'location' header.

----------------------------------------

TITLE: Print PDF/JPG File using Application Print Method (JavaScript)
DESCRIPTION: Provides a JavaScript example for using the `appObj.print` method to open a browser's print dialog for a PDF or JPG file. It fetches the file as a blob, passes it to the print options, and includes basic error handling.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/ellie-mae-scripting-objects-reference

LANGUAGE: JavaScript
CODE:
```
async function btnPrint_click(ctrl) {
  try {
    const data = await fetch('<URL_TO_PDF_OR_JPG_FILE>');
    const blob = await data.blob();
    const printOptions = { blob };
    const appObj = await elli.script.getObject('application');
    await appObj.print(printOptions);
  } catch (err) {
  	console.log('Error occurred while printing ', err);
  }
}
```

TITLE: Subscribe to Loan Milestone Webhook Event with cURL
DESCRIPTION: This cURL command demonstrates how to subscribe to the 'milestone' event for 'Loan' resources using the webhook subscription API. It requires an endpoint URL for callbacks and an authorization token.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/sdk-to-api-migration-getting-started-guide

LANGUAGE: bash
CODE:
```
curl --location 'https://api.elliemae.com/webhook/v1/subscriptions' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
--header 'Authorization: Bearer 0004gjyIsvQkdrSXVLtBqYlDHl' \
--data '{
    "events": [
        "milestone"
    ],
    "endpoint": "{{callbackURL}}",
    "resource": "Loan",
    "enableSubscription": true
}'
```

DESCRIPTION: Example JSON payload for creating a webhook subscription to receive notifications for events related to opening (initial) document audits, orders, and deliveries.

CODE:

```
    "events": [
        "openingauditcompleted",
        "openingauditfailed",
        "openingordercompleted",
        "openingorderfailed",
        "openingdeliverycompleted",
        "openingdeliveryfailed",
        "openingappenddocumentssucceeded",
```

TITLE: Application Object Methods API Documentation
DESCRIPTION: Documents various methods of the application object. `supportsAction` checks for action support, `getCapabilities` returns supported features, `keepSessionAlive` extends user sessions, `closeModal` closes modal windows, and `print` initiates printing of blob content.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/reference/ellie-mae-scripting-objects-reference

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
supportsAction(actionName: string): boolean
  actionName: The name of the action to check.
  Returns: A boolean indicating if the specified action is supported.

getCapabilities(): object
  Returns: A capabilities object indicating supported features/actions.
  Schema: {
    "supportedActions": ["string"],
    "supportedFeatures": ["string"]
  }

keepSessionAlive(): void
  Description: Extends the user session.

closeModal(): void
  Description: Closes the modal opened using openModal API.

print(printOptions: object): void
  printOptions: An object containing the content to print.
    blob: Blob object (e.g., PDF, JPG file).
  Description: Opens a browser’s print dialog with the content provided as Blob object. Use this print method to enable printing PDF files and JPG image files.
```

TITLE: SCIM User Provisioning API Endpoints and Operations
DESCRIPTION: This section provides a detailed overview of the SCIM API endpoints for user and user group management, including their paths, descriptions, and associated Postman examples. It also outlines how to configure Encompass user defaults and the supported patch operations (Add, Replace, Remove) for updating user profiles and groups.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/scim-user-provisioning-user-guide

LANGUAGE: APIDOC
CODE:
```
API Endpoints:
- POST /scim2/v1/users: Create User
  Description: Create a standalone Encompass or DDA User.
  Postman Examples:
    - POST 4a Create an Encompass user with SCIM
    - POST 4b Create an Encompass SSO user with SCIM

- GET /scim2/v1/users/{userId}: Get User
  Description: Retrieve User profiles associated with the globalUserId.
  Postman Examples:
    - GET 5b Retrieve Details for Specific User by global user id.

- GET /scim2/v1/users: Get Users
  Description: Retrieve All Users and associated User profiles.
  Postman Examples:
    - GET 1 Retrieve List of Users
    - GET 5a Retrieve Details for Specific User by username

- PATCH /scim2/v1/users/{userId}: Update User
  Description: Update User profiles associated with the globalUserId, create a DDA User and link to an existing Encompass User.
  Postman Examples:
    - PATCH 6a Modifying attributes of the user profile
    - PATCH 10 Create a DDA User and link to an existing Encompass User

- DELETE /scim2/v1/users/{userId}: Delete User
  Description: Disable a user profile associated with the globalUserId.
  Postman Examples:
    - DEL 9 Disable user

- GET /scim2/v1/groups: Get User Groups
  Description: Retrieve User Groups associated with the realm for a given schema. Supported only for Encompass product.
  Postman Examples:
    - GET 2 Retrieving Encompass User Group List

- GET /scim2/v1/groups/{groupId}: Get User Group
  Description: Retrieve members for a given group id. Supported only for Encompass product.
  Postman Examples:
    - GET 3 Retrieving Specific Encompass User Group

- PATCH /scim2/v1/groups/{groupId}: Update User Group
  Description: Update user group members for a given group id. Supported only for Encompass product. Changes outside members list must be done within Encompass Admin.
  Postman Examples:
    - PATCH 7 Assigning Encompass Users and Orgs to User Groups
    - PATCH 8 Removing Encompass Users and Orgs from User Groups

Configuring Encompass User Defaults:
  - System Administrators can configure default values for user Organization, Persona, and Working Folder attributes in Admin Tools > Server Settings Manager > Policies.
  - When defaults are configured, 'workingFolder', 'personas', and 'organization' properties are not required in the request payload for creating an Encompass user.

Updating a User or User Group:
  - When creating a request payload to update a user profile or user group, determine the type of update operation and the type of attribute/sub-attribute to be updated.

Operations (for SCIM PATCH calls):
  - Add: Use to add a new value for an attribute.
  - Replace: Use to replace existing value of an attribute.
    - For User Profile: Also re-enables a disabled user.
    - For User Group: Only supported when updating 'orgLevel' attribute where type is 'Organization'.
  - Remove: Delete the existing value of an attribute.
```

TITLE: Developer Connect API Endpoints Overview

```
DOCUMENT DELIVERY:
  Packages:
    - Get Packages (GET)
    - Get Package (GET)
  Point of Sale Integration Framework:
    - Contracts
    - Create a Package Event (POST)
    - Create a Session (POST)

Encompass Contacts:
  Borrower:
    - V1 Contracts
    - Link Borrower Contacts to a Loan
    - V1 Get Borrower Contact List (POST)
    - V1 Get a Borrower Contact (GET)
    - V1 Get Canonical Names (GET)
    - V1 View Borrower Contacts (with Pagination) (POST)
    - V1 Create Borrower Contact (POST)
    - V1 Update Borrower Contact (PATCH)
    - V1 Delete Borrower Contact (DELETE)
    - V1 Get All Notes for a Borrower Contact (GET)
    - V1 Get a Note for a Borrower Contact (GET)
    - V1 Create a Note for a Borrower Contact (POST)
    - V1 Update a Note for a Borrower Contact (PATCH)
    - V1 Delete a Note for a Borrower Contact (DELETE)
  Groups:
    - V1 Contracts
    - V1 Get All Contact Groups (GET)
    - V1 Get a Contact Group (GET)
    - V1 Get All Contacts in Contact Group (GET)
    - V1 Create Contact Group (POST)
    - V1 Add or Remove Group Contacts (PATCH)
    - V1 Update a Contact Group (PATCH)
    - V1 Delete a Contact Group (DELETE)
  Business:
    - V1 Contracts
    - Link Business Contacts to a Loan
    - V1 Get Business Contact List (POST)
    - V1 Get a Business Contact (GET)
    - V1 Get Canonical Names (GET)
    - V1 View Business Contacts (with Pagination) (POST)
    - V1 Create a Business Contact (POST)
    - V1 Update a Business Contact (PATCH)
    - V1 Delete a Business Contact (DELETE)
    - V1 Get a Note for a Business Contact (GET)
    - V1 Create Note for a Business Contact (POST)
    - V1 Update Note for a Business Contact (PATCH)
    - V1 Delete Note for a Business Contact (DELETE)
```

TITLE: Obtain Access Token using cURL
DESCRIPTION: This snippet demonstrates how to acquire an OAuth 2.0 access token using the Resource Owner Password Credentials flow. It requires plugging in specific input variables like username, password, client ID, and client secret into the request body to authenticate and receive an access token.

SOURCE: https://developer.icemortgagetechnology.com/developer-connect/docs/sdk-to-api-migration-getting-started-guide

LANGUAGE: bash
CODE:
```
curl --location 'https://api.elliemae.com/oauth2/v1/token' \
--header 'Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded' \
--data-urlencode 'grant_type=password' \
--data-urlencode 'username=admin@encompass:{{instnace_Id}}' \
--data-urlencode 'password={{password}}' \
--data-urlencode 'client_id={{Oauth clientId}}' \
--data-urlencode 'client_secret={{oauthSecret}}'
```
